294	+const NO_RATING = 'Not yet rated'
295	+const NO_PHOTOS = 'No photos for this park yet'
296	+const NOT_LISTED = 'Not listed'
297	+const NO_AMENITIES = 'No amenities listed'
298	+
299	+function NoPhoto() {
300	+  return (
301	+    <div
302	+      data-testid="no-photo"
303	+      className="flex aspect-[16/9] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] border-2 border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg p-4 text-center text-muted"
304	+    >
305	+      <svg
306	+        aria-hidden="true"
307	+        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
308	+        className="size-7"
309	+        fill="none"
310	+        stroke="currentColor"
311	+        strokeWidth="1.75"
312	+        strokeLinecap="round"
313	+        strokeLinejoin="round"
314	+      >
315	+        <rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2" />
316	+        <path d="m3 16 5-5 4 4 3-3 6 6" />
317	+        <circle cx="15.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5" />
318	+      </svg>
319	+      <p>{NO_PHOTOS}</p>
320	+    </div>
321	+  )
322	+}
323	+
324	+/** First photo only, with a "Photo 1 of N" label. A load failure swaps in the no-photo box. */
325	+function PhotoArea({ park }) {
326	+  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false)
327	+  const src = park.images[0]
328	+
329	+  if (!src || failed) return <NoPhoto />
330	+
331	+  return (
332	+    <figure className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] bg-missing-bg">
333	+      <img
334	+        src={src}
335	+        alt={`Photo of ${park.name}`}
336	+        onError={() => setFailed(true)}
337	+        className="block aspect-[16/9] w-full object-cover"
338	+      />
339	+      <figcaption className="absolute right-2 bottom-2 rounded-full bg-ink/85 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white">
340	+        Photo 1 of {park.images.length}
341	+      </figcaption>
342	+    </figure>
343	+  )
344	+}
345	+
346	+function FactTile({ label, value }) {
347	+  const { missing, text } = value
348	+  return (
349	+    <div
350	+      className={`min-w-0 break-words rounded-[10px] border p-3 ${
351	+        missing ? 'border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg' : 'border-line bg-surface'
352	+      }`}
353	+    >
354	+      <dt className="text-sm text-muted">{label}</dt>
355	+      <dd className={missing ? 'italic text-muted' : 'font-semibold'}>{text}</dd>
356	+    </div>
357	+  )
358	+}
359	+
360	+/** Full details for one normalized park. Every missing value shows its labelled fallback. */
361	+export default function ParkDetails({ park }) {
362	+  const headingRef = useRef(null)
363	+
364	+  // Opening details (or moving to another park) puts focus on the heading.
365	+  useEffect(() => {
366	+    headingRef.current?.focus()
367	+  }, [park.id])
368	+
369	+  const address = display(park.address, NO_ADDRESS)
370	+  const about = display(park.description, NO_DESCRIPTION)
371	+
372	+  return (
373	+    <article>
374	+      <Link
375	+        to="/"
376	+        className="focus-ring inline-flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 font-semibold text-forest hover:border-forest"
377	+      >
378	+        <svg
379	+          aria-hidden="true"
380	+          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
381	+          className="size-5"
382	+          fill="none"
383	+          stroke="currentColor"
384	+          strokeWidth="2"
385	+        >
386	+          <path d="m15 6-6 6 6 6" />
387	+        </svg>
388	+        All parks
389	+      </Link>
390	+
391	+      <div className="mt-4">
392	+        <PhotoArea key={park.id} park={park} />
393	+      </div>
394	+
395	+      <h1
396	+        ref={headingRef}
397	+        tabIndex={-1}
398	+        className="mt-4 text-[28px] leading-tight font-extrabold"
399	+      >
400	+        {park.name}
401	+      </h1>
402	+      <p className="mt-1 text-muted">{address.text}</p>
403	+
404	+      {park.hasCoords && (
405	+        <p className="mt-2">
406	+          <a
407	+            href={osmUrl(park.lat, park.lng)}
408	+            target="_blank"
409	+            rel="noopener noreferrer"
410	+            className="focus-ring inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded font-semibold text-forest underline"
411	+          >
412	+            Open in OpenStreetMap
413	+          </a>
414	+        </p>
415	+      )}
416	+
417	+      <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
418	+        <FactTile label="Hours" value={display(park.hours, NOT_LISTED)} />
419	+        <FactTile label="Size" value={display(formatAcres(park.acreage), NOT_LISTED)} />
420	+        <FactTile label="Rating" value={display(formatRating(park.rating), NO_RATING)} />
421	+      </dl>
422	+
423	+      <section aria-labelledby="park-about" className="mt-5">
424	+        <h2 id="park-about" className="text-lg font-bold">
425	+          About
426	+        </h2>
427	+        <p className={`mt-1 ${about.missing ? 'italic text-muted' : ''}`}>{about.text}</p>
428	+      </section>
429	+
430	+      <section aria-labelledby="park-amenities" className="mt-5">
431	+        <h2 id="park-amenities" className="text-lg font-bold">
432	+          Amenities
433	+        </h2>
434	+        {park.amenities.length > 0 ? (
435	+          <ul aria-label="Amenities" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
436	+            {park.amenities.map((token, index) => (
437	+              <li
438	+                key={`${index}-${token}`}
439	+                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-tag-bg px-3 py-1 text-tag-fg"
440	+              >
441	+                <svg
442	+                  aria-hidden="true"
443	+                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
444	+                  className="size-4"
445	+                  fill="none"
446	+                  stroke="currentColor"
447	+                  strokeWidth="2.5"
448	+                >
449	+                  <path d="m5 12 5 5 9-10" />
450	+                </svg>
451	+                {amenityLabel(token)}
452	+              </li>
453	+            ))}
454	+          </ul>
455	+        ) : (
456	+          <p className="mt-1 italic text-muted">{NO_AMENITIES}</p>
457	+        )}
458	+      </section>
459	+    </article>
460	+  )
461	+}
462	diff --git a/src/data/format.js b/src/data/format.js
463	index b16586b..5bb8421 100644
464	--- a/src/data/format.js
465	+++ b/src/data/format.js
466	@@ -3,3 +3,33 @@ export function amenityLabel(token) {
467	   const spaced = String(token ?? '').trim().replace(/-/g, ' ')
468	   return spaced.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + spaced.slice(1)
469	 }
470	+
471	+/** Rating in 0–5 as "4.7 / 5" (one decimal); `null` when there is no valid rating. */
472	+export function formatRating(n) {
473	+  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
474	+  return `${n.toFixed(1)} / 5`
475	+}
476	+
477	+/** Acreage as "526 acres" or "1 acre"; `null` when there is no valid acreage. */
478	+export function formatAcres(n) {
479	+  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
480	+  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'acre' : 'acres'}`
481	+}
482	+
483	+/** OpenStreetMap link centred on a point. */
484	+export function osmUrl(lat, lng) {
485	+  return `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat=${lat}&mlon=${lng}#map=16/${lat}/${lng}`
486	+}
487	+
488	+/**
489	+ * The one fallback rule: a value that is null, undefined or blank text is
490	+ * missing, and shows the labelled fallback instead. Never invents a value.
491	+ */
492	+export function display(value, fallback) {
493	+  const missing =
494	+    value === null ||
495	+    value === undefined ||
496	+    (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '') ||
497	+    (typeof value === 'number' && !Number.isFinite(value))
498	+  return missing ? { text: fallback, missing: true } : { text: String(value), missing: false }
499	+}

+function tile(label) {

+import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
+import { Link } from 'react-router'
+import { amenityLabel, display, formatAcres, formatRating, osmUrl } from '../data/format.js'
+
+// Fallback copy, word for word from design/README.md.
+const NO_ADDRESS = 'Address not listed'
+const NO_DESCRIPTION = 'No description has been provided for this park.'
+const NO_RATING = 'Not yet rated'
+const NO_PHOTOS = 'No photos for this park yet'
+const NOT_LISTED = 'Not listed'
+const NO_AMENITIES = 'No amenities listed'
+
+function NoPhoto() {
+  return (
+    <div
+      data-testid="no-photo"
+      className="flex aspect-[16/9] flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-[10px] border-2 border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg p-4 text-center text-muted"
+    >
+      <svg
+        aria-hidden="true"
+        viewBox="0 0 24 24"
+        className="size-7"
+        fill="none"
+        stroke="currentColor"
+        strokeWidth="1.75"
+        strokeLinecap="round"
+        strokeLinejoin="round"
+      >
+        <rect x="3" y="4" width="18" height="16" rx="2" />
+        <path d="m3 16 5-5 4 4 3-3 6 6" />
+        <circle cx="15.5" cy="8.5" r="1.5" />
+      </svg>
+      <p>{NO_PHOTOS}</p>
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+/** First photo only, with a "Photo 1 of N" label. A load failure swaps in the no-photo box. */
+function PhotoArea({ park }) {
+  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false)
+  const src = park.images[0]
+
+  if (!src || failed) return <NoPhoto />
+
+  return (
+    <figure className="relative overflow-hidden rounded-[10px] bg-missing-bg">
+      <img
+        src={src}
+        alt={`Photo of ${park.name}`}
+        onError={() => setFailed(true)}
+        className="block aspect-[16/9] w-full object-cover"
+      />
+      <figcaption className="absolute right-2 bottom-2 rounded-full bg-ink/85 px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[13px] font-semibold text-white">
+        Photo 1 of {park.images.length}
+      </figcaption>
+    </figure>
+  )
+}
+
+function FactTile({ label, value }) {
+  const { missing, text } = value
+  return (
+    <div
+      className={`min-w-0 break-words rounded-[10px] border p-3 ${
+        missing ? 'border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg' : 'border-line bg-surface'
+      }`}
+    >
+      <dt className="text-sm text-muted">{label}</dt>
+      <dd className={missing ? 'italic text-muted' : 'font-semibold'}>{text}</dd>
+    </div>
+  )
+}
+
+/** Full details for one normalized park. Every missing value shows its labelled fallback. */
+export default function ParkDetails({ park }) {
+  const headingRef = useRef(null)
+
+  // Opening details (or moving to another park) puts focus on the heading.
+  useEffect(() => {
+    headingRef.current?.focus()
+  }, [park.id])
+
+  const address = display(park.address, NO_ADDRESS)
+  const about = display(park.description, NO_DESCRIPTION)
+
+  return (
+    <article>
+      <Link
+        to="/"
+        className="focus-ring inline-flex min-h-11 min-w-11 items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-line bg-surface px-3 font-semibold text-forest hover:border-forest"
+      >
+        <svg
+          aria-hidden="true"
+          viewBox="0 0 24 24"
+          className="size-5"
+          fill="none"
+          stroke="currentColor"
+          strokeWidth="2"
+        >
+          <path d="m15 6-6 6 6 6" />
+        </svg>
+        All parks
+      </Link>
+
+      <div className="mt-4">
+        <PhotoArea key={park.id} park={park} />
+      </div>
+
+      <h1
+        ref={headingRef}
+        tabIndex={-1}
+        className="mt-4 text-[28px] leading-tight font-extrabold"
+      >
+        {park.name}
+      </h1>
+      <p className="mt-1 text-muted">{address.text}</p>
+
+      {park.hasCoords && (
+        <p className="mt-2">
+          <a
+            href={osmUrl(park.lat, park.lng)}
+            target="_blank"
+            rel="noopener noreferrer"
+            className="focus-ring inline-flex min-h-11 items-center rounded font-semibold text-forest underline"
+          >
+            Open in OpenStreetMap
+          </a>
+        </p>
+      )}
+
+      <dl className="mt-3 grid grid-cols-3 gap-2">
+        <FactTile label="Hours" value={display(park.hours, NOT_LISTED)} />
+        <FactTile label="Size" value={display(formatAcres(park.acreage), NOT_LISTED)} />
+        <FactTile label="Rating" value={display(formatRating(park.rating), NO_RATING)} />
+      </dl>
+
+      <section aria-labelledby="park-about" className="mt-5">
+        <h2 id="park-about" className="text-lg font-bold">
+          About
+        </h2>
+        <p className={`mt-1 ${about.missing ? 'italic text-muted' : ''}`}>{about.text}</p>
+      </section>
+
+      <section aria-labelledby="park-amenities" className="mt-5">
+        <h2 id="park-amenities" className="text-lg font-bold">
+          Amenities
+        </h2>
+        {park.amenities.length > 0 ? (
+          <ul aria-label="Amenities" className="mt-2 flex flex-wrap gap-2">
+            {park.amenities.map((token, index) => (
+              <li
+                key={`${index}-${token}`}
+                className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-full bg-tag-bg px-3 py-1 text-tag-fg"
+              >
+                <svg
+                  aria-hidden="true"
+                  viewBox="0 0 24 24"
+                  className="size-4"
+                  fill="none"
+                  stroke="currentColor"
+                  strokeWidth="2.5"
+                >
+                  <path d="m5 12 5 5 9-10" />
+                </svg>
+                {amenityLabel(token)}
+              </li>
+            ))}
+          </ul>
+        ) : (
+          <p className="mt-1 italic text-muted">{NO_AMENITIES}</p>
+        )}
+      </section>
+    </article>
+  )
+}
diff --git a/src/data/format.js b/src/data/format.js
index b16586b..5bb8421 100644
--- a/src/data/format.js
+++ b/src/data/format.js
@@ -3,3 +3,33 @@ export function amenityLabel(token) {
   const spaced = String(token ?? '').trim().replace(/-/g, ' ')
   return spaced.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + spaced.slice(1)
 }
+
+/** Rating in 0–5 as "4.7 / 5" (one decimal); `null` when there is no valid rating. */
+export function formatRating(n) {
+  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
+  return `${n.toFixed(1)} / 5`
+}
+
+/** Acreage as "526 acres" or "1 acre"; `null` when there is no valid acreage. */
+export function formatAcres(n) {
+  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
+  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'acre' : 'acres'}`
+}
+
+/** OpenStreetMap link centred on a point. */
+export function osmUrl(lat, lng) {
+  return `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat=${lat}&mlon=${lng}#map=16/${lat}/${lng}`
+}
+
+/**
+ * The one fallback rule: a value that is null, undefined or blank text is
+ * missing, and shows the labelled fallback instead. Never invents a value.
+ */
+export function display(value, fallback) {
+  const missing =
+    value === null ||
+    value === undefined ||
+    (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '') ||
+    (typeof value === 'number' && !Number.isFinite(value))
+  return missing ? { text: fallback, missing: true } : { text: String(value), missing: false }
+}

+describe('formatRating', () => {
+  it('shows one decimal out of 5', () => {
+    expect(formatRating(4.7)).toBe('4.7 / 5')
+    expect(formatRating(4)).toBe('4.0 / 5')

+  })
+
+  it('returns null for a missing or invalid rating', () => {
+    expect(formatRating(null)).toBeNull()
+    expect(formatRating(undefined)).toBeNull()
+    expect(formatRating(NaN)).toBeNull()
+    expect(formatRating('4.5')).toBeNull()
+  })
+})

+describe('formatAcres', () => {
+  it('pluralizes acres', () => {
+    expect(formatAcres(526)).toBe('526 acres')
+    expect(formatAcres(1)).toBe('1 acre')
+    expect(formatAcres(0.5)).toBe('0.5 acres')
+  })
+
+  it('returns null for a missing or invalid acreage', () => {
+    expect(formatAcres(null)).toBeNull()

+  })

/** Human label for a kebab-case amenity token: `dog-run` → "Dog run". Unknown tokens are humanized, never dropped. */
export function amenityLabel(token) {
  const spaced = String(token ?? '').trim().replace(/-/g, ' ')
  return spaced.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + spaced.slice(1)
}

/** Rating in 0–5 as "4.7 / 5" (one decimal); `null` when there is no valid rating. */
export function formatRating(n) {
  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
  return `${n.toFixed(1)} / 5`
}

/** Acreage as "526 acres" or "1 acre"; `null` when there is no valid acreage. */
export function formatAcres(n) {
  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'acre' : 'acres'}`
}

/** OpenStreetMap link centred on a point. */
export function osmUrl(lat, lng) {
  return `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat=${lat}&mlon=${lng}#map=16/${lat}/${lng}`
}

/**
 * The one fallback rule: a value that is null, undefined or blank text is
 * missing, and shows the labelled fallback instead. Never invents a value.
 */
export function display(value, fallback) {
  const missing =
    value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '') ||
    (typeof value === 'number' && !Number.isFinite(value))
  return missing ? { text: fallback, missing: true } : { text: String(value), missing: false }
}
import { amenityLabel, display, formatAcres, formatRating, osmUrl } from './format.js'

describe('formatRating', () => {
  it('shows one decimal out of 5', () => {
    expect(formatRating(4.7)).toBe('4.7 / 5')
    expect(formatRating(4)).toBe('4.0 / 5')
    expect(formatRating(0)).toBe('0.0 / 5')
  })

  it('returns null for a missing or invalid rating', () => {
    expect(formatRating(null)).toBeNull()
    expect(formatRating(undefined)).toBeNull()
    expect(formatRating(NaN)).toBeNull()
    expect(formatRating('4.5')).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('formatAcres', () => {
  it('pluralizes acres', () => {
    expect(formatAcres(526)).toBe('526 acres')
    expect(formatAcres(1)).toBe('1 acre')
    expect(formatAcres(0.5)).toBe('0.5 acres')
  })

  it('returns null for a missing or invalid acreage', () => {
    expect(formatAcres(null)).toBeNull()
    expect(formatAcres(undefined)).toBeNull()
    expect(formatAcres(Infinity)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('osmUrl', () => {
  it('builds the OpenStreetMap link from lat/lng', () => {
    expect(osmUrl(40.6602, -73.969)).toBe(
      'https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat=40.6602&mlon=-73.969#map=16/40.6602/-73.969',
    )
  })
})

describe('display', () => {
  it('passes a present value through as text', () => {
    expect(display('Dawn to dusk', 'Not listed')).toEqual({ text: 'Dawn to dusk', missing: false })
    expect(display(0, 'Not listed')).toEqual({ text: '0', missing: false })
  })

  it('uses the fallback for null, undefined, blank text and NaN', () => {
    for (const value of [null, undefined, '', '   ', NaN]) {
      expect(display(value, 'Not listed')).toEqual({ text: 'Not listed', missing: true })
    }
  })
})

describe('amenityLabel', () => {
  it('replaces dashes with spaces and capitalizes the first letter', () => {
    expect(amenityLabel('dog-run')).toBe('Dog run')
    expect(amenityLabel('wildlife-viewing')).toBe('Wildlife viewing')
  })

  it('capitalizes single-word tokens', () => {
    expect(amenityLabel('wifi')).toBe('Wifi')
  })

  it('humanizes unknown tokens instead of dropping them', () => {
    expect(amenityLabel('zip-line-tower')).toBe('Zip line tower')
  })

  it('keeps the rest of the token as written', () => {
    expect(amenityLabel('BBQ-pits')).toBe('BBQ pits')
  })

  it('trims surrounding whitespace before transforming', () => {
    expect(amenityLabel(' dog-run ')).toBe('Dog run')
  })
})
// Null-safe park data layer. Normalization never invents values and never
// mutates the parsed source: missing scalars become `null`, missing arrays `[]`.

const imageModules = import.meta.glob('../assets/park-images/*.png', {
  eager: true,
  query: '?url',
  import: 'default',
})

// filename stem (e.g. "prospect-1") -> bundled asset URL
const bundledImages = Object.fromEntries(
  Object.entries(imageModules).map(([path, url]) => [fileStem(path), url]),
)

function fileStem(pathOrUrl) {
  const withoutQuery = pathOrUrl.split(/[?#]/)[0]
  const file = withoutQuery.split('/').pop() ?? ''
  const dot = file.lastIndexOf('.')
  return dot > 0 ? file.slice(0, dot) : file
}

/** Map a source image URL to the bundled art with the same filename stem; unmatched URLs pass through unchanged. */
export function resolveImageUrl(url) {
  if (typeof url !== 'string') return url
  return bundledImages[fileStem(url)] ?? url
}

function text(value) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return null
  const trimmed = value.trim()
  return trimmed !== '' ? trimmed : null
}

function finiteNumber(value) {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
}

function numberInRange(value, min, max) {
  const n = finiteNumber(value)
  return n !== null && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null
}

function stringList(value) {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item) => text(item) !== null) : []
}

export function normalizePark(raw) {
  const park = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {}
  const location = park.location && typeof park.location === 'object' ? park.location : {}
  const lat = numberInRange(location.lat, -90, 90)
  const lng = numberInRange(location.lng, -180, 180)
  const acreage = finiteNumber(park.acreage)
  const rating = finiteNumber(park.rating)

  return {
    id: text(park.id),
    name: text(park.name),
    number: null,
    description: text(park.description),
    address: text(location.address),
    lat,
    lng,
    hasCoords: lat !== null && lng !== null,
    amenities: stringList(park.amenities),
    hours: text(park.hours),
    images: stringList(park.images).map(resolveImageUrl),
    acreage: acreage !== null && acreage >= 0 ? acreage : null,
    rating: rating !== null && rating >= 0 && rating <= 5 ? rating : null,
  }
}

function byName(a, b) {
  if (a.name === null) return b.name === null ? 0 : 1
  if (b.name === null) return -1
  return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })
}

/** Normalize, sort A–Z by name and number 1–N. The number is assigned once here and shared by every view. */
export function normalizeParks(rawParks) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawParks)) return []
  return rawParks
    .filter((raw) => raw && typeof raw === 'object')
    .map(normalizePark)
    .sort(byName)
    .map((park, index) => ({ ...park, number: index + 1 }))
}

  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`)
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to load parks: HTTP ${response.status}`)
import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router'
import ParkDetails from './components/ParkDetails.jsx'
import ParkList from './components/ParkList.jsx'
import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'

const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'

function NotFound() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
      <p className="mt-2">
        <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
          Back to all parks
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function ParkDetailsRoute({ parks, onOpen }) {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)

  // Record the opened park so the list can return focus to its card.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (park) onOpen(park.id)
  }, [park, onOpen])

  if (!park) return <NotFound />

  return <ParkDetails park={park} />
}

function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry, lastParkId, setLastParkId }) {
  const clearLastParkId = useCallback(() => setLastParkId(null), [setLastParkId])

  if (status === 'loading') {
    return (
      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
        Loading parks…
      </p>
    )
  }

  if (status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={retry}
          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
        >
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <ParkList parks={parks} focusParkId={lastParkId} onFocusUsed={clearLastParkId} />
        }
      />
      <Route
        path="/parks/:id"
        element={<ParkDetailsRoute parks={parks} onOpen={setLastParkId} />}
      />
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  )
}

/** Human label for a kebab-case amenity token: `dog-run` → "Dog run". Unknown tokens are humanized, never dropped. */
export function amenityLabel(token) {
  const spaced = String(token ?? '').trim().replace(/-/g, ' ')
  return spaced.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + spaced.slice(1)
}

/** Rating in 0–5 as "4.7 / 5" (one decimal); `null` when there is no valid rating. */
export function formatRating(n) {
  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
  return `${n.toFixed(1)} / 5`
}

/** Acreage as "526 acres" or "1 acre"; `null` when there is no valid acreage. */
export function formatAcres(n) {
  if (typeof n !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(n)) return null
  return `${n} ${n === 1 ? 'acre' : 'acres'}`
}

/** OpenStreetMap link centred on a point. */
export function osmUrl(lat, lng) {
  return `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat=${lat}&mlon=${lng}#map=16/${lat}/${lng}`
}

/**
 * The one fallback rule: a value that is null, undefined or blank text is
 * missing, and shows the labelled fallback instead. Never invents a value.
 */
export function display(value, fallback) {
  const missing =
    value === null ||
    value === undefined ||
    (typeof value === 'string' && value.trim() === '') ||
    (typeof value === 'number' && !Number.isFinite(value))
  return missing ? { text: fallback, missing: true } : { text: String(value), missing: false }
}

// Null-safe park data layer. Normalization never invents values and never
// mutates the parsed source: missing scalars become `null`, missing arrays `[]`.

const imageModules = import.meta.glob('../assets/park-images/*.png', {
  eager: true,
  query: '?url',
  import: 'default',
})

// filename stem (e.g. "prospect-1") -> bundled asset URL
const bundledImages = Object.fromEntries(
  Object.entries(imageModules).map(([path, url]) => [fileStem(path), url]),
)

function fileStem(pathOrUrl) {
  const withoutQuery = pathOrUrl.split(/[?#]/)[0]
  const file = withoutQuery.split('/').pop() ?? ''
  const dot = file.lastIndexOf('.')
  return dot > 0 ? file.slice(0, dot) : file
}

/** Map a source image URL to the bundled art with the same filename stem; unmatched URLs pass through unchanged. */
export function resolveImageUrl(url) {
  if (typeof url !== 'string') return url
  return bundledImages[fileStem(url)] ?? url
}

function text(value) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string') return null
  const trimmed = value.trim()
  return trimmed !== '' ? trimmed : null
}

function finiteNumber(value) {
  return typeof value === 'number' && Number.isFinite(value) ? value : null
}

function numberInRange(value, min, max) {
  const n = finiteNumber(value)
  return n !== null && n >= min && n <= max ? n : null
}

function stringList(value) {
  return Array.isArray(value) ? value.filter((item) => text(item) !== null) : []
}

export function normalizePark(raw) {
  const park = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? raw : {}
  const location = park.location && typeof park.location === 'object' ? park.location : {}
  const lat = numberInRange(location.lat, -90, 90)
  const lng = numberInRange(location.lng, -180, 180)
  const acreage = finiteNumber(park.acreage)
  const rating = finiteNumber(park.rating)

  return {
    id: text(park.id),
    name: text(park.name),
    number: null,
    description: text(park.description),
    address: text(location.address),
    lat,
    lng,
    hasCoords: lat !== null && lng !== null,
    amenities: stringList(park.amenities),
    hours: text(park.hours),
    images: stringList(park.images).map(resolveImageUrl),
    acreage: acreage !== null && acreage >= 0 ? acreage : null,
    rating: rating !== null && rating >= 0 && rating <= 5 ? rating : null,
  }
}

function byName(a, b) {
  if (a.name === null) return b.name === null ? 0 : 1
  if (b.name === null) return -1
  return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })
}

/** Normalize, sort A–Z by name and number 1–N. The number is assigned once here and shared by every view. */
export function normalizeParks(rawParks) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawParks)) return []
  return rawParks
    .filter((raw) => raw && typeof raw === 'object')
    .map(normalizePark)
    .sort(byName)
    .map((park, index) => ({ ...park, number: index + 1 }))
}

export async function loadParks() {
  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`)
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to load parks: HTTP ${response.status}`)
  }
  const data = await response.json()
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
    throw new Error('Failed to load parks: expected an array')
  }
  return normalizeParks(data)
}

## Park card (list)

- Number badge, park name (17px bold), address, and the first three amenities as tags. If there are more, add a "+N more" tag.
- The whole card is a single `<button>` (or a link once there is routing), so it gets keyboard focus and one Tab stop.

## Details

The details view shows, from top to bottom:

1. Photo area (see the missing-data rules below)
2. Park name as the page heading (26–28px, 800 weight), then the address
3. An **Open in OpenStreetMap** link built from the park's lat/lng (`https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat={lat}&mlon={lng}#map=16/{lat}/{lng}`). This was added during design and is easy to drop if you don't want it.
4. Three fact tiles: **Hours**, **Size** (acres), **Rating** ("4.7 / 5")
5. **About** (the description)
6. **Amenities**: all of them, as tags with a check mark. Turn the raw values into labels by replacing `-` with a space and capitalising the first letter (`dog-run` → "Dog run").

## Missing data: never invent a value

The brief requires this, and the sample data has every one of these cases.

| Field | When missing / null / empty | Show |
|---|---|---|
| `location.address` | Highland Dog Park | "Address not listed" in the secondary text colour |
| `description` | Old Mill Botanical Garden (`null`) | "No description has been provided for this park." |
| `rating` | Cedar Hill (`null`) | "Not yet rated" (do not show 0 or blank stars) |
| `images` | Cedar Hill, East Ridge (`[]`) | A dashed box: image icon + "No photos for this park yet" |
| `images` failing to load | **Every** sample URL (`images.example.com` doesn't exist) | The same fallback, triggered by `onError` on `<img>`; never a broken-image icon |
| `hours` | not in the sample, but handle it | "Not listed" |
| `acreage` | not in the sample, but handle it | "Not listed" |
| `amenities` | not in the sample, but handle it | Hide the Amenities section, or show "No amenities listed" |
| `location.lat/lng` | not in the sample, but handle it | Keep the park in the list, leave it off the map, and hide the map link |

Fact tiles with a missing value use the muted look from screen 07: `#F0EDE5` background, dashed `#C9C4B6` border, italic text. *Remark: the desktop screenshot (03) shows "Not yet rated" with the normal tile style. That is a mistake in the mock, and the phone version is the intended look.*

Wrap every field in a small helper (e.g. `display(value, fallback)`) so the rules stay in one place. Never let a missing nested field crash the app (`park.location?.address`).

## Design tokens (Tailwind)

Add these under `theme.extend` (or as CSS variables with Tailwind v4 `@theme`):

  missing: { bg: '#F0EDE5', line: '#C9C4B6' },       // missing-value tiles, no-photo box

  const lat = numberInRange(location.lat, -90, 90)
  const lng = numberInRange(location.lng, -180, 180)
  const acreage = finiteNumber(park.acreage)
  const rating = finiteNumber(park.rating)

  return {
    id: text(park.id),
    name: text(park.name),
    number: null,
    description: text(park.description),
    address: text(location.address),
    lat,
    lng,
    hasCoords: lat !== null && lng !== null,
    amenities: stringList(park.amenities),
    hours: text(park.hours),
    images: stringList(park.images).map(resolveImageUrl),
    acreage: acreage !== null && acreage >= 0 ? acreage : null,
    rating: rating !== null && rating >= 0 && rating <= 5 ? rating : null,
  }
}

function byName(a, b) {
  if (a.name === null) return b.name === null ? 0 : 1
  if (b.name === null) return -1
  return a.name.localeCompare(b.name, 'en', { sensitivity: 'base' })
}

/** Normalize, sort A–Z by name and number 1–N. The number is assigned once here and shared by every view. */
export function normalizeParks(rawParks) {
  if (!Array.isArray(rawParks)) return []
  return rawParks
    .filter((raw) => raw && typeof raw === 'object')
    .map(normalizePark)
    .sort(byName)
    .map((park, index) => ({ ...park, number: index + 1 }))
}

export async function loadParks() {
  const response = await fetch(`${import.meta.env.BASE_URL}data/parks.sample.json`)
  if (!response.ok) {
    throw new Error(`Failed to load parks: HTTP ${response.status}`)
  }
  const data = await response.json()
  if (!Array.isArray(data)) {
    throw new Error('Failed to load parks: expected an array')
  }
  return normalizeParks(data)
}

import { useCallback, useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link, Route, Routes, useParams } from 'react-router'
import ParkDetails from './components/ParkDetails.jsx'
import ParkList from './components/ParkList.jsx'
import { useParks } from './data/useParks.js'

const cardClass = 'rounded-[10px] border border-line bg-surface p-4'
const linkClass = 'focus-ring rounded text-forest underline'

function NotFound() {
  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-lg font-bold">Park not found</h1>
      <p className="mt-2">
        <Link to="/" className={linkClass}>
          Back to all parks
        </Link>
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

function ParkDetailsRoute({ parks, onOpen }) {
  const { id } = useParams()
  const park = parks.find((p) => p.id === id)

  // Record the opened park so the list can return focus to its card.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (park) onOpen(park.id)
  }, [park, onOpen])

  if (!park) return <NotFound />

  return <ParkDetails park={park} />
}

function ParksPanel({ status, parks, retry, lastParkId, setLastParkId }) {
  const clearLastParkId = useCallback(() => setLastParkId(null), [setLastParkId])

  if (status === 'loading') {
    return (
      <p role="status" className={cardClass}>
        Loading parks…
      </p>
    )
  }

  if (status === 'error') {
    return (
      <div role="alert" className={cardClass}>
        <p>Couldn't load parks. Try again.</p>
        <button
          type="button"
          onClick={retry}
          className="focus-ring mt-3 min-h-11 rounded-lg bg-forest px-4 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-forest-dark"
        >
          Retry
        </button>
      </div>
    )
  }

  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/"
        element={
          <ParkList parks={parks} focusParkId={lastParkId} onFocusUsed={clearLastParkId} />
        }
      />
      <Route
        path="/parks/:id"
        element={<ParkDetailsRoute parks={parks} onOpen={setLastParkId} />}
      />
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  )
}

export default function App() {
  const { status, parks, retry } = useParks()
  // Id of the last-opened park. Lives in App state (not location.state) so
  // focus can return to its card after browser Back, which carries no state.
  const [lastParkId, setLastParkId] = useState(null)

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen flex-col">
      <header className="bg-forest px-5 py-4 text-white">
        <p className="text-xl font-extrabold">Find a Park</p>
        <p className="text-sm">New York City area</p>
        {status === 'ready' && <p className="text-sm">{parks.length} parks</p>}
      </header>
      <main className="flex flex-1 flex-col">
        <aside aria-label="Parks" className="p-5">
          <ParksPanel
            status={status}
            parks={parks}
            retry={retry}
            lastParkId={lastParkId}
            setLastParkId={setLastParkId}
          />
        </aside>
        <section aria-label="Map of parks" className="flex-1 p-5">
          <p className="text-muted">Map coming soon.</p>
        </section>
      </main>
    </div>
  )
}

title: 'Story 1.3: Park details with missing-data handling'
type: 'feature'
created: '2026-10-14'
baseline_commit: '643096771a75679e78ad0f58484d3afd9c26144c'

<frozen-after-approval reason="human-owned intent — do not modify unless human renegotiates">

## Intent

**Problem:** `/parks/:id` shows only the park name (`ParkDetailsPlaceholder`). Residents can't see hours, size, rating, description, photos or amenities, or tell when a value is missing rather than wrong (CAP-3).

**Approach:** Replace the placeholder with a `ParkDetails` view in this order: an "All parks" back link, the photo area, the name as `<h1>`, the address, an "Open in OpenStreetMap" link, a `<dl>` of Hours/Size/Rating tiles, About, and Amenities. Every missing value gets the labelled fallback from `design/README.md`. Focus moves to the `<h1>` when details open.

## Boundaries & Constraints

**Always:** render the normalized park exactly as given and never invent a value; fallback copy matches the design table word for word; missing-value tiles use `bg-missing-bg`, a dashed `border-missing-line` and italic text; `focus-ring` and ≥ 44px targets on the back link and the OSM link; reuse `amenityLabel` from `src/data/format.js`; keep the `NotFound` branch and the `onOpen` id recording.

**Never:** a photo gallery or carousel (first photo only); map, markers or marker highlight (1.4); the desktop sidebar/phone header layout split or the List/Map toggle (1.5); an "Open now" badge or hours parsing; star icons; clickable `<div>`s; editing `parks.sample.json` or the normalizer.

**Decisions (agent-made, user-invisible or design-permitted):** empty amenities shows the Amenities heading with "No amenities listed" rather than hiding it. Rating shows one decimal (`4` → "4.0 / 5"). Size shows "N acres" ("1 acre" when N is 1). Photo alt is "Photo of {name}". The back control is a `<Link to="/">`, so the existing `lastParkId` mechanism returns focus to the card.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Full data | Prospect Park | all sections; "Photo 1 of 2"; tiles "6:00 AM - 1:00 AM", "526 acres", "4.7 / 5"; 7 checked amenity tags | N/A |
| Missing address | Highland Dog Park | "Address not listed" in `text-muted` | N/A |
| Null description | Old Mill Botanical Garden | About reads "No description has been provided for this park." | N/A |
| Null rating | Cedar Hill | Rating tile "Not yet rated", muted style; no "0" | N/A |
| No images | Cedar Hill, East Ridge | dashed "No photos for this park yet" box, no `<img>` | N/A |
| Image fails | `<img>` fires `error` | the no-photo box replaces it | no broken-image icon |
| Missing hours / acreage | synthetic park | the tile reads "Not listed", muted style | N/A |
| Empty amenities | synthetic `amenities: []` | "No amenities listed" | N/A |
| No coordinates | synthetic `hasCoords: false` | no OSM link | N/A |
| Unknown amenity | `wifi`, `dog-run` | "Wifi", "Dog run" | humanized, never dropped |
| Back control | click "All parks" | `/` renders; that park's card has focus | N/A |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map

- `src/App.jsx` -- `ParkDetailsPlaceholder` (find park by `:id`, call `onOpen`, `NotFound` fallback) is the code to replace. Move that lookup into the new component, or wrap it. `linkClass`/`cardClass` are reusable styles. `lastParkId` + `ParkList focusParkId` already restore card focus on any return to `/`.
- `src/data/parks.js` -- normalized park `{ id, name, number, description, address, lat, lng, hasCoords, amenities, hours, images, acreage, rating }`. Missing values are `null`/`[]`, `rating` is in 0–5 or `null`, and `images` already resolve to bundled assets. Don't change it.
- `src/data/format.js` -- `amenityLabel`; add the small display helpers here (the epic asks for a central `display(value, fallback)`-style rule set).
- `src/index.css` -- tokens `missing-bg`, `missing-line`, `tag-bg`, `tag-fg`, `muted`, `forest`, `line`, `surface`; the `focus-ring` utility.
- `src/App.test.jsx` -- the `renderAt(path)`, `okResponse()` (structuredClone of the sample, so tests can mutate it for synthetic parks) and `TestNav` patterns.
- `design/screens/02-desktop-park-selected.png`, `06-phone-details.png`, `07-phone-missing-data.png` -- the visuals: bordered "‹ All parks" button, a rounded photo with a dark "Photo 1 of N" pill at bottom-right, a 3-column tile grid, ✓ tags.
- OSM URL: `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat={lat}&mlon={lng}#map=16/{lat}/{lng}`.

## Tasks & Acceptance

**Execution:**
- [x] `src/data/format.js` -- add `formatRating(n)` → "4.7 / 5" or `null`, `formatAcres(n)` → "526 acres" / "1 acre" or `null`, `osmUrl(lat, lng)`, and `display(value, fallback)` → `{ text, missing }` so the view applies every fallback through one rule.
- [x] `src/components/ParkDetails.jsx` -- `ParkDetails({ park })` renders the sections in order: `PhotoArea` (keyed by park id, `onError` → no-photo box), an `<h1 tabIndex={-1}>` focused on mount, a `<dl>` of `FactTile`s (muted variant when the value is missing), About, and Amenities as a `<ul>` of ✓ tags.
- [x] `src/App.jsx` -- the `/parks/:id` route renders `ParkDetails` for a found park (keep `onOpen` and `NotFound`); delete `ParkDetailsPlaceholder`.
- [x] `src/data/format.test.js`, `src/App.test.jsx` -- unit-test the new helpers; test every matrix row (synthetic rows by editing the cloned sample in a custom fetch mock), section order, and focus on the heading after opening from the list.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given the list, when the user opens a park by keyboard, then the details `<h1>` has focus and is the only `<h1>` in the view.
- Given details, when the user activates "All parks", then the list renders and focus is on that park's card.
- Given a park with images, then one `<img>` shows with alt naming the park and a "Photo 1 of N" label.
- Given any of the 12 sample parks, when details render, then nothing crashes and no value reads "null", "undefined" or "NaN".

## Implementation Notes

## Spec Change Log

## Review Triage Log

## Design Notes

Focus-to-heading uses a ref plus `useEffect` keyed on `park.id`, so moving from one park to another also refocuses. The heading needs `tabIndex={-1}` to be focusable from code, and it gets no `focus-ring`, since a programmatic focus target is not a Tab stop. `PhotoArea` keeps a `failed` state and is keyed by park id, so a failure on one park does not hide another park's photo.

Missing-value tile golden example:

```jsx
<div className={missing ? 'border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg' : 'border-line bg-surface'}>
  <dt className="text-sm text-muted">Rating</dt>
  <dd className={missing ? 'italic text-muted' : 'font-semibold'}>{value ?? fallback}</dd>
</div>
```

- `npm test` -- expected: all tests pass.
- `npm run build` -- expected: succeeds with no errors.

**Manual checks (if no CLI):**
- `npm run dev`. Open Cedar Hill: dashed photo box and a muted "Not yet rated" tile. Open Prospect Park: the photo shows "Photo 1 of 2". Tab to "All parks", press Enter, and focus lands on the card.

Rating shows one decimal (`4` → "4.0 / 5"). Size shows "N acres" ("1 acre" when N is 1). Photo alt is "Photo of {name}". The back control is a `<Link to="/">`, so the existing `lastParkId` mechanism returns focus to the card.

## I/O & Edge-Case Matrix

| Scenario | Input / State | Expected Output / Behavior | Error Handling |
|----------|--------------|---------------------------|----------------|
| Full data | Prospect Park | all sections; "Photo 1 of 2"; tiles "6:00 AM - 1:00 AM", "526 acres", "4.7 / 5"; 7 checked amenity tags | N/A |
| Missing address | Highland Dog Park | "Address not listed" in `text-muted` | N/A |
| Null description | Old Mill Botanical Garden | About reads "No description has been provided for this park." | N/A |
| Null rating | Cedar Hill | Rating tile "Not yet rated", muted style; no "0" | N/A |
| No images | Cedar Hill, East Ridge | dashed "No photos for this park yet" box, no `<img>` | N/A |
| Image fails | `<img>` fires `error` | the no-photo box replaces it | no broken-image icon |
| Missing hours / acreage | synthetic park | the tile reads "Not listed", muted style | N/A |
| Empty amenities | synthetic `amenities: []` | "No amenities listed" | N/A |
| No coordinates | synthetic `hasCoords: false` | no OSM link | N/A |
| Unknown amenity | `wifi`, `dog-run` | "Wifi", "Dog run" | humanized, never dropped |
| Back control | click "All parks" | `/` renders; that park's card has focus | N/A |

</frozen-after-approval>

## Code Map

- `src/App.jsx` -- `ParkDetailsPlaceholder` (find park by `:id`, call `onOpen`, `NotFound` fallback) is the code to replace. Move that lookup into the new component, or wrap it. `linkClass`/`cardClass` are reusable styles. `lastParkId` + `ParkList focusParkId` already restore card focus on any return to `/`.
- `src/data/parks.js` -- normalized park `{ id, name, number, description, address, lat, lng, hasCoords, amenities, hours, images, acreage, rating }`. Missing values are `null`/`[]`, `rating` is in 0–5 or `null`, and `images` already resolve to bundled assets. Don't change it.
- `src/data/format.js` -- `amenityLabel`; add the small display helpers here (the epic asks for a central `display(value, fallback)`-style rule set).
- `src/index.css` -- tokens `missing-bg`, `missing-line`, `tag-bg`, `tag-fg`, `muted`, `forest`, `line`, `surface`; the `focus-ring` utility.
- `src/App.test.jsx` -- the `renderAt(path)`, `okResponse()` (structuredClone of the sample, so tests can mutate it for synthetic parks) and `TestNav` patterns.
- `design/screens/02-desktop-park-selected.png`, `06-phone-details.png`, `07-phone-missing-data.png` -- the visuals: bordered "‹ All parks" button, a rounded photo with a dark "Photo 1 of N" pill at bottom-right, a 3-column tile grid, ✓ tags.
- OSM URL: `https://www.openstreetmap.org/?mlat={lat}&mlon={lng}#map=16/{lat}/{lng}`.

## Tasks & Acceptance

**Execution:**
- [x] `src/data/format.js` -- add `formatRating(n)` → "4.7 / 5" or `null`, `formatAcres(n)` → "526 acres" / "1 acre" or `null`, `osmUrl(lat, lng)`, and `display(value, fallback)` → `{ text, missing }` so the view applies every fallback through one rule.
- [x] `src/components/ParkDetails.jsx` -- `ParkDetails({ park })` renders the sections in order: `PhotoArea` (keyed by park id, `onError` → no-photo box), an `<h1 tabIndex={-1}>` focused on mount, a `<dl>` of `FactTile`s (muted variant when the value is missing), About, and Amenities as a `<ul>` of ✓ tags.
- [x] `src/App.jsx` -- the `/parks/:id` route renders `ParkDetails` for a found park (keep `onOpen` and `NotFound`); delete `ParkDetailsPlaceholder`.
- [x] `src/data/format.test.js`, `src/App.test.jsx` -- unit-test the new helpers; test every matrix row (synthetic rows by editing the cloned sample in a custom fetch mock), section order, and focus on the heading after opening from the list.

**Acceptance Criteria:**
- Given the list, when the user opens a park by keyboard, then the details `<h1>` has focus and is the only `<h1>` in the view.
- Given details, when the user activates "All parks", then the list renders and focus is on that park's card.
- Given a park with images, then one `<img>` shows with alt naming the park and a "Photo 1 of N" label.
- Given any of the 12 sample parks, when details render, then nothing crashes and no value reads "null", "undefined" or "NaN".

## Implementation Notes

## Spec Change Log

## Review Triage Log

## Design Notes

Focus-to-heading uses a ref plus `useEffect` keyed on `park.id`, so moving from one park to another also refocuses. The heading needs `tabIndex={-1}` to be focusable from code, and it gets no `focus-ring`, since a programmatic focus target is not a Tab stop. `PhotoArea` keeps a `failed` state and is keyed by park id, so a failure on one park does not hide another park's photo.

Missing-value tile golden example:

```jsx
<div className={missing ? 'border-dashed border-missing-line bg-missing-bg' : 'border-line bg-surface'}>
  <dt className="text-sm text-muted">Rating</dt>
  <dd className={missing ? 'italic text-muted' : 'font-semibold'}>{value ?? fallback}</dd>
</div>
```

Every other field goes through `display()` with a labelled fallback; the name doesn't. No test covers a nameless park. The \"no null/undefined/NaN\" test reads `article.textContent`, which excludes attributes, so it would miss the bad `alt`.\n- **The missing-address test proves nothing, and a missing address isn't marked as missing.** The address `<p>` always has `text-muted`, whether or not the address is present. So `expect(getByText('Address not listed')).toHaveClass('text-muted')` would pass even if the fallback had no styling of its own. Missing description, missing amenities and missing tiles all get `italic`; the missing address looks exactly like a real one. Consider using `address.missing` the same way `about.missing` is used, and assert the difference in the test.\n- **When the first photo fails, the other photos are never tried** (`PhotoArea`). Only `park.images[0]` is attempted. If it fails, the no-photo box says \"No photos for this park yet\" even when the park has more URLs, which is a false statement. The design rule is to never invent a value. Also, \"Photo 1 of N\" counts URLs that were never checked. Either move to the next image on error, or change the wording or count. Add a test with several images where the first one fails.\n- **`formatAcres` doesn't format large or fractional numbers** (`src/data/format.js`). `1500` shows as `\"1500 acres\"` with no thousands separator. `0.3333333` shows as `\"0.3333333 acres\"` with no rounding. Something like `toLocaleString('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 1 })` would fix both. The tests only cover 526, 1 and 0.5.\n- **`display()` turns any non-string value into text without checking it.** An object or array becomes `\"[object Object]\"` or `\"a,b\"`, which breaks the \"never show garbage\" rule. Today's callers pass values that were already normalized. But this is described as \"the one fallback rule\" and is exported for reuse, so it should either treat non-primitive values as missing or document that it only accepts strings and numbers. The `display` tests don't cover objects, arrays or booleans.\n- **The \"Open in OpenStreetMap\" link doesn't say it opens a new tab.** It uses `target=\"_blank\"` but has no visible or screen-reader hint (such as \"(opens in new tab)\" or an icon with hidden text). Keyboard and screen-reader users are moved to a new context without warning.\n- **The details route never updates `document.title`.** The tab title stays the same on every park, so screen-reader users and browser history can't tell parks apart. This page doesn't set a title anywhere.\n- **The three fact tiles are forced into one row at every width** (`grid grid-cols-3`). At the 390px phone width in the design, each tile is about 110px wide. A value like `\"6:00 AM - 1:00 AM\"` or `\"Not yet rated\"` will wrap into several cramped lines. Consider a responsive layout (e.g. `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-3`, or `auto-fit`). No test or layout check covers phone width.\n- **The amenities name is announced twice.** The `<ul aria-label=\"Amenities\">` sits inside a `<section aria-labelledby=\"park-amenities\">` whose heading already says \"Amenities\", so screen readers read the label twice. The tests rely on the list's accessible name, so changing it means updating `amenityLabels()`.\n- **`data-testid=\"no-photo\"` is never used.** `NoPhoto` sets it, but every test finds the box by its text. Either use it or remove it.\n- **Duplicate amenities aren't removed.** Tokens like `\"dog-run\"` and `\"Dog run\"`, or a repeated token, show as duplicate tags. The index-based `key` hides this from React's duplicate-key warning. A label-level dedupe, plus a test, would cover it.\n- **The diff leaves out the story's spec and status files.** The branch's staged changes include `_bmad-output/implementation-artifacts/spec-1-3-park-details-with-missing-data-handling.md` and `sprint-status.yaml`, but the reviewed diff covers only `src/`. The `TestNav` helpers at lines 99 and 293 only go to `/parks/prospect-park`, `/parks/nope`, `/` or back.\n- **Missing verification:** A test that opens park A, fires `error` on its image, navigates directly to park B (which has images), and asserts:\n  - B's `<img>` renders.\n  - B's `<h1>` has focus.\n- **Demonstration:** Remove `key={park.id}` from `PhotoArea`, or change the effect deps to `[]`. No test fails: every details test mounts a single park, so neither the remount nor the re-run of the effect is ever exercised.\n- **Consequence:** Once direct park-to-park navigation exists, a photo error on one park can hide the next park's photo, and focus can stay on the old heading position.\n- **Disposition:** `defer`. In the current UI there is no direct park-to-park navigation: the details view links only to `/`, and history always passes through `/`. This becomes reachable with map selection in story 1.4, which is the natural place for a `TestNav`-style A→B test.\n\n## Other findings\n\n- In `src/data/format.js`, the docstring for `display` says the rule covers \"null, undefined or blank text\", but the code also treats non-finite numbers as missing.   - **Changed surface:** Two design-documented behaviors (spec "Design Notes"):
    - `useEffect(() => headingRef.current?.focus(), [park.id])`, which refocuses the heading when moving from one park to another.
    - `<PhotoArea key={park.id} …>`, so that "a failure on one park does not hide another park's photo".
    - Both are in `src/components/ParkDetails.jsx` (diff lines ~365-367 and ~392).

    - B's `<h1>` has focus.

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""
merge_session_transcripts.py

Collect the main transcript and every subagent transcript for a Claude Code
session ID, then interleave them into one timeline sorted by timestamp.

Claude Code stores sessions under ~/.claude/projects/<project-slug>/:
    <session-id>.jsonl                       main conversation
    <session-id>/subagents/agent-*.jsonl     each subagent's full transcript
    <session-id>/**/*.jsonl                  any other agent transcripts (picked up too)

Usage:
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id>
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id> -f md -o session.md
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id> -f jsonl -o merged.jsonl
    python3 merge_session_transcripts.py <session-id> --projects-dir /path/to/.claude/projects

Formats:
    text   readable plain text (default)
    md     Markdown, one heading per message
    jsonl  the original JSON entries, merged, each tagged with "_source"
"""

import argparse
import json
import sys
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from pathlib import Path

DEFAULT_PROJECTS_DIR = Path.home() / ".claude" / "projects"
MIN_TS = datetime.min.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- discovery

def find_session_files(session_id: str, projects_dir: Path):
    """Return (main_file or None, [agent files]) for the session ID."""
    mains = sorted(projects_dir.glob(f"*/{session_id}.jsonl"))
    session_dirs = sorted(p for p in projects_dir.glob(f"*/{session_id}") if p.is_dir())

    if not mains and not session_dirs:
        return None, []

    if len(mains) > 1:
        print(f"warning: session found in {len(mains)} project folders; using all of them",
              file=sys.stderr)

    agent_files = []
    for d in session_dirs:
        agent_files.extend(sorted(d.rglob("*.jsonl")))

    return (mains, agent_files)


def source_label(path: Path, session_id: str) -> str:
    if path.name == f"{session_id}.jsonl":
        return "main"
    stem = path.stem  # e.g. agent-a1b2c3
    meta = path.with_suffix(".meta.json")
    if meta.exists():
        try:
            m = json.loads(meta.read_text())
            kind = m.get("agentType") or m.get("subagent_type") or m.get("type")
            if kind:
                return f"{stem} ({kind})"
        except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
            pass
    return stem


# ---------------------------------------------------------------- loading

def parse_ts(value):
    if not value or not isinstance(value, str):
        return None
    try:
        dt = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
        return dt if dt.tzinfo else dt.replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)
    except ValueError:
